- **tt-post-job**: Post a role to Teamtailor as a fully-configured DRAFT by copying the "Atomic Template" job (internal new_from_template endpoint) and applying per-role edits: the CLIENT PORTAL's JD (get_role.job_description), Client, recruiter, salary range, tailored locations, role-corrected trigger texts, the kit, the ⌖ questions, and the portal's candidate-page link in the Scheduled-stage message.
